- **defence-bypass**: Defence bypass testing for the control between the caller's input and the effect, covering matcher gaps when a filter lists shapes, decode disagreements when a pipeline normalises the value, structural re-reading when a component reads one member, permit enumeration gaps when a policy lists what may run, tier gaps when one layer holds the verdict, and accounting gaps when a limit counts one dimension or one request unit. - **defence-bypass-allowance-gap**: Allowance gap bypass where a permit enumerates what may happen, covering policy gaps when a directive or source list leaves a route to execution, sanitiser enumeration gaps when allowed elements or attributes combine into the effect, secret permit values when the key the policy relies on can be read or reused, and confinement gaps when a declared restriction has an unlisted route to the same capability. ([Diekgbbtt/polyphemus](https://github.com/Diekgbbtt/polyphemus/tree/HEAD/skills/web-vulnerability-testing/defence-bypass/defence-bypass-allowance-gap) · ★1)
- **defence-bypass-attempt-batching**: Attempt batching where a limit counts requests while the backend iterates a payload, covering array bodies when a list of values is tested in one counted request, alias sets when one operation envelope repeats a field under distinct names, multipart batches when inner operations ride an envelope the limiter does not parse, and fat requests when a body is consumed on a verb the counter treats as one read. ([Diekgbbtt/polyphemus](https://github.com/Diekgbbtt/polyphemus/tree/HEAD/skills/web-vulnerability-testing/defence-bypass/defence-bypass-attempt-batching) · ★1)
- **defence-bypass-edge-origin-differential**: Edge-origin differential bypass where a control is enforced at one tier and the effect served at another, covering route divergence when a legacy or alternate route skips the current chain, verdict reuse when a stored decision is applied to a changed request, and scope divergence when the enforcing tier covers less than the serving tier. ([Diekgbbtt/polyphemus](https://github.com/Diekgbbtt/polyphemus/tree/HEAD/skills/web-vulnerability-testing/defence-bypass/defence-bypass-edge-origin-differential) · ★1)
- **defence-bypass-encoding-differential**: Encoding differential bypass between the checking side and the consuming side of one value, covering decode-count gaps when each hop decodes a different number of times, folding gaps when normalisation maps two spellings onto one value, coercion or notation equivalence when a comparison folds a value the sink treats as distinct, and truncation or padding forms when the accepting side tolerates a shortened value. ([Diekgbbtt/polyphemus](https://github.com/Diekgbbtt/polyphemus/tree/HEAD/skills/web-vulnerability-testing/defence-bypass/defence-bypass-encoding-differential) · ★1)
- **defence-bypass-parser-confusion**: Parser confusion bypass between two readers of one request, covering duplicate member precedence when the control reads one occurrence and the sink another, framing and content-type reading when each side consumes a different representation, framing-declaration obfuscation when a normalising front end re-derives the boundary, and verb or location re-reading when the gate checks a member the executor overrides. ([Diekgbbtt/polyphemus](https://github.com/Diekgbbtt/polyphemus/tree/HEAD/skills/web-vulnerability-testing/defence-bypass/defence-bypass-parser-confusion) · ★1)
- **defence-bypass-signature-evasion**: Signature evasion past a matcher's rule set, covering token splitting and separator substitutes when the list matches literal keywords, comment and whitespace insertion when the list matches contiguous shapes, equivalent operators when the list matches only the obvious spelling, and depth padding or alternate grammar forms when the matcher counts nesting or lists one element spelling.
